- **react-components**: React + UI conventions for apps/web (MUI v7 + Tailwind). Use when building or editing a React component — structure, theme colors, memoization, filter state, and Spanish-only UI text. ([undp/carbon-footprint-program](https://github.com/undp/carbon-footprint-program/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/react-components))
- **react-components**: How components and hooks are written in the portfolio — UI primitives vs feature components vs routes, render-only components with logic extracted into hooks, prop design (unions over booleans), React 19 idioms (ref as prop, &lt;title&gt;, Actions), composition, error boundaries and Suspense. Load BEFORE writing or modifying any .tsx component or custom hook. ([William-Stoops/Portfolio](https://github.com/William-Stoops/Portfolio/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/react-components))
